  an iPhone viewport run in the daily suite, experimental WebKit and real

  dates checked against `/api/report/room/{id}`, and no counts or seed values
  used as expected results. Cleanup can run twice safely and treats a 404 as
  already cleaned up. A mid-run reset is classed as an environment issue. Tests
  run one at a time with at most 1 retry.

› Message from test-architect
  I wrote the architecture handoff, and its status is READY FOR IMPLEMENTATION.
  Nothing is blocking. Only that one file was changed: no installs, no HTTP
  requests, no commit. It runs to 438 lines, a bit over the 250-400 target,
  because of the bullet layout. I left it rather than restructure.

  **Subset:** 9 core tests in 5 spec files, plus 2 stretch tests in 2 more
  files. Tiers are picked by folder: smoke is a subset of hotfix, which is a
  subset of daily.

  | # | Spec file | Test | Tags |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 1 | `smoke/api-contracts.cy.ts` | S-01 room list contract | @p0 @readonly
  @smoke |
  | 2 | `smoke/api-contracts.cy.ts` | S-03 anonymous booking list gets 401 | @p0
  @readonly @smoke |
  | 3 | `smoke/home.cy.ts` | S-07 home shows the rooms (desktop) | @p0 @readonly
  @smoke |
  | 4 | `smoke/home.cy.ts` | S-08 the same at iPhone viewport | @p0 @readonly
  @smoke |
  | 5 | `smoke/reservation-page.cy.ts` | S-09 reservation form loads (no submit;
  the spike pins the assertion) | @p0 @readonly @smoke |
  | 6 | `hotfix/guest-booking.cy.ts` | S-10 guest books a stay through the UI on
  the real backend (desktop) | @p0 @write @hotfix |
  | 7 | `hotfix/guest-booking.cy.ts` | S-11 the same at iPhone viewport | @p0
  @write @hotfix |
  | 8 | `daily/booking-api.cy.ts` | S-31 booking lifecycle (see below) | @p0
  @write @daily |
  | 9 | `daily/booking-api.cy.ts` | S-14 invalid booking gets 400 with the field
  rule messages | @p1 @write @daily |
  | Stretch 1 | `daily/admin-login.cy.ts` | S-20 admin UI login | — |
  | Stretch 2 | `daily/contact-message.cy.ts` | S-17 contact message | — |

  - **S-31 is new and evidence-backed.** It folds in S-12 and S-15: create 201,
  same booking again 409, admin GET by id and by room list, report includes the
  window, delete 202, then GET 404.
  - **Writes and cleanup:** each write test creates at most one tagged booking.
  Its id goes into the cleanup registry immediately after the 201, before any
  assertion, and afterEach deletes it, treating 404 as already gone.
  - **Time:** core test-writing is about 1:18, and the full implementation
  checklist is planned at 2:02.
  - **Deferred:** the other catalogue scenarios are listed with reasons. The
  handoff also states that read-only smoke does not prove booking, and that
  iPhone viewport does not validate Safari/iOS.

  **Review of D-006..D-010:**
  - **D-006, accept with revision:**
    - The hotfix gate runs the booking at desktop and iPhone viewport (S-10 and
  S-11), so two bookings instead of one.
    - The tiers map to CI triggers: pull request = smoke, manual dispatch =
  hotfix, schedule = daily.
    - In CI, missing admin credentials make the run fail instead of skipping.
    - The daily wording now says only the UI guest journeys run at both
  viewports; API and admin checks run once.
  - **D-007, accept with revision:** the viewport is an explicit 390x844,
  applied to S-08 and S-11. The iOS user agent is used only if Cypress allows it
  per suite, which is not verified. S-11 is in the hotfix gate.
  - **D-008, accept with revision:**
    - The title format is `[S-xx][R-yy] ... @pN @readonly|@write @tier`.
    - Cypress's built-in JUnit reporter writes one XML file per spec.
    - The README holds the traceability matrix until Xray keys exist.
    - The Xray import stays NOT VERIFIED.
  - **D-009, accept with revision:**
    - Delete only records we can prove are ours: the id from our own 201, or a
  message whose subject exactly matches our run tag (message creation returns no
  id). Never sweep by tag pattern.
    - The tag is `qa` plus 8 lowercase letters, used as the last name.
    - The date window keeps a 1-day buffer around booked ranges.
    - Write tests need the admin credentials for cleanup.
    - A failed cleanup is logged, not thrown, so it does not abort the spec.
  - **D-010, accept.** Optionally add one line: no GraphQL and no Newman.

  **New decisions proposed:**
  - **D-011:** tiers are chosen by spec folder globs, so no `@cypress/grep`
  dependency.
  - **D-012:** admin auth is an API login that sets the `token` cookie inside
  `cy.session`, validated through `/api/auth/validate`.
    - The session id is the constant `'admin'`.
    - Auth and cleanup requests don't log and assert the status only, so
  credentials and the token stay out of JUnit files and screenshots.
    - Credentials come only from env or CI secrets.
  - **D-013:** GitHub Actions is the reference pipeline (daily schedule, manual
  dispatch with a tier input, smoke on pull requests, serialized runs, artifact
  upload). The Bitbucket/Jenkins mapping is one paragraph.
  - **D-014:** the built-in JUnit reporter, one XML per spec.
  - **D-015:** exact pins of cypress 16.1.1 and typescript 7.0.2, checked by a
  5-minute compatibility gate. If that fails, the fallback is typescript 5.9.x.
  CI uses Node 24.
  - **D-016:** the two findings (message count readable without login, logout
  not revoking the token) go in the README and DECISIONS.md, not into tests.
  - **D-017:** the UI booking test checks the network first (request body, the
  201 and the booking id). On-screen text is asserted only once it has been
  seen. Any fallback that rewrites the request dates must be labelled.

  **Unknowns that don't block:**
  - **U-1 and U-2:** whether the reservation page takes its dates from the query
  string, and the confirmation text. Handled by a 10-minute spike with
  fallbacks A/B/C.
  - **U-3:** admin UI selectors. This only matters for stretch 1.
  - **U-4:** TypeScript 7 with the Cypress loader is not verified; the
  compatibility gate covers it.
  - **U-5:** the Cypress 16 env API and the per-suite user agent override. Both
  are checked during scaffolding.

  Admin credentials have to be provided at run time, through the shell or the
  git-ignored `cypress.env.json`.

  Two notes for the implementer:
  - The GitHub workflow only triggers once `delivery/part1-test-suite/` becomes
  the root of its own repo.
  - Human time spent so far has not been provided, so the time budget is a plan
  only.

› Message from test-automation-writer
  Phase A is finished: typecheck passes (exit 0) and the cleanup proofs pass 8/8
  (exit 0). Nothing has run live, so every behaviour claim below is still
  unverified until Phase B. No Cypress run, no requests to the demo, no
  installs, no commits. I did not touch the root package-lock.json, README, 03

  **Files changed** (all under `/Users/joaopedrobarbosa/Documents/techInterviewS
  NDB/.claude/worktrees/qa-lead-take-home/delivery/part1-test-suite/`)
  - `cypress/support/pages/reservation.ts`: replaces the synthetic `body` drag
  with a real CDP pointer drag on the calendar cells, and adds the
  initial-window helper and the selection proof. `openForm` and `fillGuest` are
  unchanged.
  - `cypress/e2e/hotfix/guest-booking.cy.ts`: S-10 starts in
  `'inside-target-month'`, S-11 in `'outside-target-month'`.
  `recordBookingObservation` now records the test title, retry, time and initial
  window. The business assertions are unchanged.
  - `cypress/support/e2e.ts`: rewritten with no `uncaught:exception` handler.
  Only the `drainObservedCreates` registration and `cleanupCurrentAttempt`
  remain.
  - `cypress/support/node-tasks.ts`: removes the #418 symptom recording and
  accepts the new observation fields. `failureSymptom` now maps the new tagged
  messages instead of the old `cy.trigger` ones.
  - The edit script was kept outside the repo at
  `/tmp/qa-phaseA-scripts/apply-edits.mjs`. It makes exact-match replacements
  and fails if a line is not found exactly once.

  **Mechanism: CDP, no new dependency**
  - Each step sends `Cypress.automation('remote:debugger:protocol', { command:
  'Input.dispatchMouseEvent', ... })`, in the RCA order: move to start, press on
  the check-in cell, 12 moves with `buttons:1`, release on the last-night cell.
  - It first asserts `Cypress.isBrowser({family:'chromium'})`.
  - Iframe offset and scale: it finds `iframe.aut-iframe` in the top window. I
  confirmed that class in Cypress 15.5's runner bundle, read-only. It asserts
  that iframe's `contentWindow` is the AUT window.
    - `scale = iframeRect.width / aut.innerWidth`, and it checks the height
  ratio agrees within 0.01.
    - The top-page point is `iframeRect.left + x*scale`, `iframeRect.top +
  y*scale`.
  - Geometry steps, in order:
    1. Navigate with Next, asserting the toolbar label after every click.
    2. Scroll the target row to the centre with native
  `scrollIntoView({block:'center'})`.
    3. Wait until the month view rect and scroll position read the same 3 times
  in a row.
    4. Re-read the cell rects in the same `.then` that sends the input.
  - Hit check before pressing: at both AUT-local points (bottom-centre minus 4px
  of each `.rbc-day-bg`), `elementFromPoint` must be inside `.rbc-month-view`,
  in the target `.rbc-month-row`, and not on an `.rbc-event`.
  - A passive listener confirms a trusted mousedown reached the target row and a
  trusted mouseup reached the app. It is removed afterwards.
  - No `force`, no `cy.on('fail')`. Failures carry tags: `[native-pointer]`,
  `[calendar-hit-target]`, `[calendar-geometry]`, `[calendar-precondition]`,
  `[calendar-selection]`.
  - `cypress-real-events` is not needed unless CDP fails live.

  **Initial windows**
  - `initialWindow()` picks the URL window. Inside the month: 7 days earlier, or
  14 days later when the check-in day is 1-7. That always lands in the same
  month on a different week row.
  - Outside: 60 days earlier, which can never appear in the target month view.
  - I did not use your "minus 14" example. Generated dates are Mondays on day 25
  or earlier, and for check-in days up to 14 a 14-day offset would fall outside
  the visible month.
  - The smoke spec calls `reservationUrl(room.roomid, dates)` and gets the
  inside window by default.

  **Selection-proof assertions**
  - Before the drag, each Selected segment is recorded as row index, first/last
  column and pixel left/right. It uses the `.rbc-row-segment` wrapper, whose
  width maps onto the day columns.
    - S-11 asserts there are 0 Selected segments in the target month view.
    - S-10 asserts at least one is visible and none already matches the target.
  - After the drag, inside a retrying `should`:
    - exactly one Selected segment;
    - its row equals the row containing the check-in;
    - `|left - checkinCell.left| <= 2`;
    - `|right - lastNightCell.right| <= 2`;
    - `[first,last]` equals `[checkinCol, lastNightCol]`;
    - it does not match any pre-drag segment.
  - The generic "Selected exists" check is gone.

  **Business assertions:** room, names, deposit, `deep.eq(dates)` on the
  request, real 201, numeric ID, echoed identity and dates, "Booking Confirmed",
  `.booking-card strong` showing `${checkin} - ${checkout}`, and "Return home"
  are all unchanged. The observation now has `test` (titlePath), `attempt`
  (currentRetry), `observedAt` and `initialWindow`.

  **#418:** the handler is gone and there is no `uncaught:exception` handler
  anywhere; grep finds only the explanatory comment. Also removed:
  `hydrationErrors`, the `recordBrowserSymptoms` call and task, the
  `browserSymptoms` array and its summary field, and the branch that turned a
  passing run into `PASS WITH RISKS` because of symptoms. `PASS WITH RISKS` now
  comes only from cleanup problems, and FAIL still takes precedence. The
  `'react-hydration-418'` failure label stays, so #418 failures are named in the
  summary.

  **Cleanup is unchanged in behaviour:**
  - Full identity GET before DELETE, and a 404 check after it.
  - The positive safe-integer ID guard.
  - Unresolved obligations reported separately, and the registry throw in
  `after:run`.
  - `tests/cleanup.test.mjs` needed no changes.

  **Validation** (run from WT)
  - `scripts/collect-evidence.sh qa-lead-take-home phaseA-typecheck npm --prefix
  delivery/part1-test-suite run typecheck`: exit 0. Evidence:
  `/Users/joaopedrobarbosa/Documents/techInterviewSNDB/.claude/worktrees/qa-lead

  t/20261009-005247-phaseA-cleanup-proofs.txt`

  **Risks for the live run**
  1. **#418 will likely fail S-10/S-11 now.** It appeared once per booking
  attempt and twice per home test, so S-10/S-11 and other UI tests may fail on
  it. That is the intended outcome; do not restore suppression.
  2. **CDP in Electron.** Cypress's server is compiled into a snapshot file, so
  I could not inspect the `remote:debugger:protocol` handler. I could only
  confirm the AUT iframe class. If CDP fails, the error will carry the
  `[native-pointer]` tag. The fallback is `cypress-real-events`, a new
  dependency that needs your approval.
  3. **Scale and coordinates in headless mode.** Points may be fractional. A
  wrong offset would show up as a missing trusted-mousedown failure rather than
  a misplaced click.
  4. **Edge tolerance.** I assumed the `.rbc-row-segment` wrapper lines up
  exactly with the cells. The RCA saw the inner event inset about 2px on the
  left, which is why I measure the wrapper. If the wrapper is also offset, the
  ±2px left check could fail.
  5. **The check-in-row hit check could catch the sticky header.** The navbar
  might cover the row; scrolling to centre should prevent that, and if it does
  not the failure is tagged `[calendar-hit-target]`.
  6. **Navigation and preconditions.** Up to 37 Next clicks, each
  label-asserted, so failures will be explicit. Two unconfirmed assumptions:
  Selected text could include extra content, and the URL Selected event may not
  render in the inside case. Either fails S-10 with a `[calendar-precondition]`
  tag.
  7. **Stability wait.** It needs 3 identical reads at Cypress's retry interval,
  which is weaker than the RCA's 50ms spacing.

  The README still describes the #418 toleration; it is due for update in Phase
  B.

  ## Exact allowance code (guest-booking.cy.ts)
  ```ts
  const ALLOWED_MESSAGE = 'Minified React error #418; visit
  https://react.dev/errors/418?args[]=HTML&args[]=';
  const ALLOWED_STACK_SOURCE = '/_next/static/chunks/174b7k13ybrt2.js';
  const CHUNK_FRAME = /\/_next\/static\/chunks\/[^\s):]+/g;
  let records: AppErrorRecord[] = [];

  function allowOneReact418PerLoad() {
    let loads = 0;
    let matchesThisLoad = 0;
    cy.on('window:before:load', () => { matchesThisLoad = 0; loads++; });
    cy.on('uncaught:exception', (err: Error) => {
      const message = err?.message ?? '';
      const stack = err?.stack ?? '';
      const frames = stack.match(CHUNK_FRAME) ?? [];
      const messageMatched = message.includes(ALLOWED_MESSAGE);
      const stackSourceMatched = frames.length ?
  stack.includes(ALLOWED_STACK_SOURCE) : null;
      const allowed = messageMatched && stackSourceMatched !== false &&
  matchesThisLoad === 0;
      if (messageMatched) matchesThisLoad++;
      records.push({ test: Cypress.currentTest.titlePath, attempt:
  Cypress.currentRetry, load: loads,
        at: new Date().toISOString(), messageMatched, stackSourceMatched,
  firstChunkFrame: frames[0] ?? null, allowed });
      if (allowed) return false;
      return undefined; // any other error, wrong stack source, or 2nd match in
  same load -> test fails
    });
  }
  // in describe:
  afterEach(() => { const batch = records; records = [];
  cy.task('recordAllowedAppErrors', batch, { log: false }); });
  ```
  - `allowOneReact418PerLoad()` is called in each test after `cy.intercept` and
  before `cy.visit`.
  - The code comment cites `evidence 20261009-418diag-*, phase B XML`, the
  runner-induced hypothesis and the removal condition.
  - Every record carries `messageMatched`. For an unrelated error it is `false`,
  not `true`, so the record says honestly why it was rejected.

  ## Risks for phase B
  1. The price summary is asserted after `openForm()`, the order you specified.
  If the live card hides or re-renders the price block once the form is open,
  the test fails at `assertPriceSummary`. Do not weaken the check; check where
  the block renders.
  2. The Total regex assumes "Total" and `£{total}` sit in the same element's
  text (e.g. sibling cells or spans in one row) with no other text between them.
  3. The `firstChunkFrame` regex expects a `/_next/static/chunks/<file>` path in
  `err.stack`. If Cypress rewrites stack URLs to a different form,
  `stackSourceMatched` is `null` and the error is allowed under the
  no-chunk-frames rule. The record shows `null`, so it stays visible in
  run-summary and the CLI.
  4. `failureSymptom`'s ' nights' match is broad, but it only affects the label.
  5. Allowed and rejected records both go through `recordAllowedAppErrors`, and
  any record sets PASS WITH RISKS. A rejected record also fails its test, so
  FAIL still takes precedence.
  6. Nothing has been executed live. The README marks the S-10/S-11 result as
  pending execution.

  What the reduced journey checks:
  - It opens the reservation page with the target dates in the URL. It never
    touches the calendar, rewrites requests or changes app state.
  - Before submitting, it checks the "£price x 2 nights" line and the total. The
    page shows no other date text without using the calendar.
  - It fills the guest form, watches the real booking request, and asserts the
    room, guest fields and exact dates.
  - It asserts the real 201 response with the echoed booking, then "Booking
    Confirmed" with the exact dates and "Return home".
  - Calendar date selection is recorded as deferred coverage: a broken calendar
    picker would not be caught.

  The #418 allowance (D-019, temporary):
  - It applies only inside the two booking tests, per test, and accepts at most
    one exact #418 message per page load from the observed app chunk.
  - Any other error, or a second #418, still fails the test.
  - Each occurrence is printed in the CLI and saved in the run summary. Both
    runs recorded exactly one per test, and the stack came from the expected
    chunk.
  - Masking risk: a real hydration bug with the same message would be hidden in
    these two tests. It's removed once #418 stops occurring under Cypress or
    developers fix the mismatch. No ticket or owner was invented.

  checklists rather than approval meetings. The PR-level smoke appears in the

  - **Real iPhone checks:** included in the release gate for UI-affecting
  changes and in the hotfix gate for user-facing fixes. A real-iPhone checklist
  is in the 60-day plan, and there is no device farm until the iPhone risks are
  proven.
  - **Post-deploy:** a smoke on production-safe checks, with a named failure
  owner agreed before the release. Hotfixes add post-deploy verification and a
  follow-up test afterwards.
  - **Metrics:** the four suggested metrics plus qualitative feedback. Each has
  a baseline collected in the first 30 days and a comparison at 3 months. No
  current numbers or targets are given; success means moving in the direction
  agreed with the team.
  - **No big migration or bureaucracy:** question 4 covers this, with a one-line
  reason for each of the five items.
